# Known-concept scores

Each cell: test pair accuracy (share of matched pairs ordered correctly by the concept vector) [95% bootstrap interval over games], and after `s` the shuffled-label control: one shuffled LP, so one cell can sit far from 0.50 for a strong concept, in either direction; read its family mean in the table under each family. `board` is the LP on a one-hot board: the same fit, no model. `random-init` is an untrained model of the same architecture. Chance is 0.50.

147 concepts; 2 listed concepts dropped for too few positives, 1301 openings likewise.
